\textstyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|{\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\widehat{\Pi}-\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})(x)}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\|{\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\widehat{\Pi}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbb{P}}^{\prime}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}}^{\prime})(x^{\prime})}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big]

(432)

\textstyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\|{\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\widehat{\Pi}-\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})(x)}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}\big]=\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}[V_{n}].

(433)

Moreover, we have

\textstyle\mathcal{U}

\textstyle=\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{i_{1}},X_{i_{2}},X_{i_{3}},X_{i_{4}})]

(434)

\textstyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\big(g_{Y}(Y_{1},Y_{2})+g_{Y}(Y_{3},Y_{4})-g_{Y}(Y_{1},Y_{3})-g_{Y}(Y_{2},Y_{4})\big)

(435)

\textstyle\quad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{1},Z_{2})+g_{Z}(Z_{3},Z_{4})-g_{Z}(Z_{1},Z_{3})-g_{Z}(Z_{2},Z_{4})\big)\big]

(436)

\textstyle=4(\Pi\times\Pi)(g_{Y}g_{Z})+4((\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\times(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}))(g_{Y}g_{Z})

(437)

\textstyle\quad-4(\Pi\times(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}))(g_{Y}g_{Z})-4((\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\times\Pi)(g_{Y}g_{Z})=4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}).

(438)

F.2
Proof of

Lem.

15
:
Lemma 15

(
\psi_{1}^{\prime}
bound)
.

By Jensen’s inequality and
Cauchy Schwarz,

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\psi_{1}^{\prime}&\textstyle=\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{E}[\overline{h}_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{1},X_{2},X_{3},X_{4})\mid X_{1}])\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq\frac{1}{4!}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4})\in\mathbf{i}_{4}^{4}}\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{i_{1}},X_{i_{2}},X_{i_{3}},X_{i_{4}})\mid X_{1}])\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\mathrm{Var}(\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{1},X_{2},X_{3},X_{4})\mid X_{1}])\leq\mathbb{E}[(\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{1},X_{2},X_{3},X_{4})\mid X_{1}])^{2}]\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\mathbb{E}[(2\iint\mathbf{k}(x,x^{\prime})\,d(\delta_{X_{1}}+\Pi-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{1}})(x)d(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(x^{\prime}))^{2}]\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq 4\mathbb{E}[\|{\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\delta_{X_{1}}+\Pi-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{1}})(x)}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}]\|{\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(x)}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}.\end{split}

The advertised result now follows from the observation that

\textstyle\big\|\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(x)\big\|_{\mathbf{k}}=\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}),

(439)

and that

\textstyle\mathbb{E}[\big\|\int\mathbf{k}(x,\cdot)\,d(\delta_{X_{1}}+\Pi-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{1}})(x)\big\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}]

(440)

\textstyle=\mathbb{E}[\iint\mathbf{k}(x,x^{\prime})\,d(\delta_{X_{1}}+\Pi-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{1}})(x)\,d(\delta_{X_{1}}+\Pi-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{1}})(x^{\prime})]

(441)

\textstyle=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{1},X_{2},X_{3},X_{4})\mid X_{1}]]=\xi.

(442)

F.3
Proof of

Lem.

16
:
Lemma 16

(
\tilde{\psi}_{1}^{\prime}
bound)
.

By the definition
Sec.

4.2
of
\tilde{\psi}_{1}^{\prime}
, we can write

\textstyle\tilde{\psi}_{1}^{\prime}

\textstyle=\mathbb{E}\big[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{2},Y_{3},Y_{4})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{2}},Z_{\pi_{3}},Z_{\pi_{4}})

(443)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{5},Y_{6},Y_{7})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{5}},Z_{\pi_{6}},Z_{\pi_{7}})\big]

(444)

\textstyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}\big[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{2},Y_{3},Y_{4})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{2}},Z_{\pi_{3}},Z_{\pi_{4}})\big]

(445)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{E}\big[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{5},Y_{6},Y_{7})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{5}},Z_{\pi_{6}},Z_{\pi_{7}})\big].

(446)

The result now follows immediately from the following lemma.

Lemma 17

(Permuted
h_{\mathrm{in}}
moments)
.

Instantiate the assumptions of
Corollary

3
, and suppose
\pi
is an independent wild bootstrap permutation of
[n]
. Then

\textstyle\mathbb{E}\big[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{2},Y_{3},Y_{4})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{2}},Z_{\pi_{3}},Z_{\pi_{4}})\big]=\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}).

(447)

If, in addition,
n\geq 14
, then

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle&\textstyle\mathbb{E}\big[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{2},Y_{3},Y_{4})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{5},Y_{6},Y_{7})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\quad\times h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{2}},Z_{\pi_{3}},Z_{\pi_{4}})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{5}},Z_{\pi_{6}},Z_{\pi_{7}})\big]\leq 4\tilde{\xi}_{\textup{wild}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}).\end{split}

Proof.

Since
n\geq 8
,
(\pi_{1},\pi_{2},\pi_{3},\pi_{4})
are independent. Hence,
equation (
447
) follows from equation (
392
) of
Lem.

13
and
Lem.

14
.

Now suppose that
n\geq 14
, so that
|[7]\cup\{\texttt{swap}(i):i\in[7]\}|=14
, and define the
\textnormal{Unif}(\{0,1\})
variables
\epsilon_{i}\triangleq\mathbf{1}_{i=\pi_{i}}
for
i\in[7]
and

\mu_{i}\triangleq\epsilon_{i}\Pi+(1-\epsilon_{i})\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}
.
We have

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle&\textstyle\mathbb{E}[h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{2},Y_{3},Y_{4})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Y}}(Y_{1},Y_{5},Y_{6},Y_{7})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{2}},Z_{\pi_{3}},Z_{\pi_{4}})h_{\mathrm{in},\mathrm{Z}}(Z_{\pi_{1}},Z_{\pi_{5}},Z_{\pi_{6}},Z_{\pi_{7}})\mid\pi,Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}}]\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\iiiint\mathbf{k}((y,z),(y^{\prime},z^{\prime}))\mathbf{k}((y^{\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime}),(y^{\prime\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime\prime}))\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad d(\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{4}-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}})(y,z)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad d(\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(y^{\prime},z^{\prime})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad d(\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{7}-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}})(y^{\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad d(\mu_{5}+\mu_{6}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(y^{\prime\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime\prime})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq\big\|\iint\mathbf{k}((y,z),\cdot)\mathbf{k}((y^{\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime}),\cdot)\,d(\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{4}-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}})(y,z)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad d(\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{7}-\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}})(y^{\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime})\big\|_{\mathbf{k}\otimes\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\times\big\|\iint\mathbf{k}((y^{\prime},z^{\prime}),\cdot)\mathbf{k}((y^{\prime\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime\prime}),\cdot)\,d(\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(y^{\prime},z^{\prime})\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad d(\mu_{5}+\mu_{6}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(y^{\prime\prime\prime},z^{\prime\prime\prime})\big\|_{\mathbf{k}\otimes\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{4}}{2},\frac{\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}+\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}}}{2}\bigg)\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg(\frac{\delta_{(Y_{1},Z_{\pi_{1}})}+\mu_{7}}{2},\frac{\delta_{Y_{1}}\times\mathbb{Q}+\mathbb{P}\times\delta_{Z_{\pi_{1}}}}{2}\bigg)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\quad\times(\epsilon_{2}+\epsilon_{3})(\epsilon_{5}+\epsilon_{6})\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\end{split}

by Cauchy-Schwarz. Here,
\|\cdot\|_{\mathbf{k}\otimes\mathbf{k}}
denotes the RKHS norm of the tensor product RKHS
\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{k}}\otimes\mathcal{H}_{\mathbf{k}}
.
The result now follows by taking expectations and applying Cauchy-Schwarz.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of

Thm.

4
:
Theorem 4

(Power of cheap independence: bounded, PSD)
.

We will establish the claim using the quantile comparison method (
Proposition

2
) by identifying complementary quantile bounds (CQBs)
\Phi_{n},\Psi_{\mathbb{X}},\Psi_{n}
satisfying the tail bounds
42
,
43
, and
44
for the test statistic
V_{n}
, auxiliary sequence
(V_{n}^{\pi_{b},s})_{b=1}^{B}
, and population parameter
\mathcal{T}=4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})
.

G.1
Bounding the fluctuations of the test statistic

We begin by identifying a CQB
\Phi_{n}
satisfying the test statistic tail bound
42
.
Our proof makes use of two lemmas. The first shows that functions of independent variables concentrate around their mean when coordinatewise function differences are bounded.

Lemma 18

(Bounded differences inequality
[
44
, Thm. D.8]
)
.

Suppose that, for all
x_{1}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{1},\,x_{2}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{2},\,\ldots,\,x_{n}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{n}
and
i\in[n]
, a function
f:{\mathcal{X}}_{1}\times{\mathcal{X}}_{2}\times\cdots\times{\mathcal{X}}_{n}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
satisfies

\textstyle\sup_{x_{i}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}_{i}}|f(x_{1},\dots,x_{i-1},x_{i},x_{i+1},\dots,x_{n})-f(x_{1},\dots,x_{i-1},x_{i}^{\prime},x_{i+1},\dots,x_{n})|\leq c_{i}.

(448)

If
X_{1},X_{2},\dots,X_{n}
are independent with each

X_{i}\in{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
then,
with probability at least
1-\delta
,

\textstyle f(X_{1},\ldots,X_{n})-\mathbb{E}[f(X_{1},\ldots,X_{n})]<\sqrt{\frac{1}{2}\log(1/\delta)\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}^{2}}.

(449)

The second lemma uses
Lem.

18
to derive a suitable CQB for
V_{n}
.

Lemma 19

(Bounded independence quantile bound)
.

Under the assumptions of
Thm.

4
,

\textstyle\mathrm{Pr}\big(|4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})-V_{n}|\geq\Phi_{n}(\beta)\big)\leq\beta\quad\text{for all}\quad\beta\in(0,1)

(450)

where

\Phi_{n}(\beta)\triangleq\Lambda^{2}(\beta)+4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\Lambda(\beta)

and

\Lambda(\beta)\triangleq\frac{6\sqrt{K}+6\sqrt{2K\log(1/\beta)}}{\sqrt{n}}
.

Proof.

Let
\tilde{X}=(\tilde{Y},\tilde{Z})
be an independent draw from
\Pi
.
By

[
51
, App. B]
,

\textstyle V_{n}

\textstyle=\frac{1}{n^{4}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4})\in[n]^{4}}h_{\mathrm{in}}(X_{i_{1}},X_{i_{2}},X_{i_{3}},X_{i_{4}})=4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\widehat{\Pi},\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}}).

(451)

By the triangle inequality, we have the following sure inequalities for each
k\in[n]
:

\textstyle|\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})-\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\widehat{\Pi},\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})|\leq\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\widehat{\Pi})+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q},\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})

(452)

\textstyle\qquad\triangleq\Delta(\mathbb{X})+\Delta^{\prime}(\mathbb{X}),

(453)

\textstyle|\Delta(\mathbb{X})-\Delta((\mathbb{X}_{-k},\tilde{X}))|\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\delta_{X_{k}},\delta_{\tilde{X}})\leq\frac{2\sqrt{K}}{n},\quad\text{and}\quad

(454)

\textstyle|\Delta^{\prime}(\mathbb{X})-\Delta^{\prime}((\mathbb{X}_{-k},\tilde{X}))|

(455)

\textstyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n}\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Y}}(\delta_{Y_{k}},\delta_{\tilde{Y}})\sqrt{(\widehat{\mathbb{Q}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})g_{Z}}+\frac{1}{n}\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Z}}(\delta_{Z_{k}},\delta_{\tilde{Z}})\sqrt{\sup_{y}g_{Y}(y,y)}\leq\frac{4\sqrt{K}}{n}.

(456)

Furthermore, by Jensen’s inequality, we have

\textstyle\mathbb{E}[\Delta(\mathbb{X})]

\textstyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\widehat{\Pi})^{2}]}=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}((\delta_{X_{i}}-\Pi)\times(\delta_{X_{i}}-\Pi))\mathbf{k}]}

(457)

\textstyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbf{k}(X_{i},X_{i})]}\leq\sqrt{\frac{K}{n}}.

(458)

The triangle inequality, Cauchy-Schwarz, and analogous reasoning also yield

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\mathbb{E}[\Delta^{\prime}(\mathbb{X})]&\textstyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q},\mathbb{P}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})]+\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\mathbb{P}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}},\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})]\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{P})g_{Y}}\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Z}}(\mathbb{Q},\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})]+\mathbb{E}[\sqrt{(\widehat{\mathbb{Q}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})g_{Z}}\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Y}}(\mathbb{P},\widehat{\mathbb{P}})]\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{P})g_{Y}]\mathbb{E}[\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Z}}^{2}(\mathbb{Q},\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[(\widehat{\mathbb{Q}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})g_{Z}\mathrm{MMD}_{g_{Y}}^{2}(\mathbb{P},\widehat{\mathbb{P}})]}\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{K}{n}}.\end{split}

Hence, by
Lem.

18
, with probability at least
1-\beta
,

\textstyle|2\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})-2\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\widehat{\Pi},\widehat{\mathbb{P}}\times\widehat{\mathbb{Q}})|<\Lambda_{V}(\beta).

(459)

Since
|a^{2}-b^{2}|=|a-b||b+a|\leq|a-b|(|b-a|+2|a|)
for all real
a,b
the result follows.
∎

G.2
Alternative representation of the independence V-statistic

We next define an alternative, equivalent way to represent the independence V-statistic (
Def.

3
) and its permuted versions, due to

[
24
]
.
We will leverage this view of the V-statistic when deriving CQBs for permuted V-statistics in
Sec.

G.3
.
For
i\in[n]
and
j\in[n]
, we define

\textstyle\bar{K}_{ij}

\textstyle\triangleq g_{Y}(Y_{i},Y_{j})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Y}(Y_{i},Y_{j^{\prime}})\quad\text{and}\quad\bar{L}_{ij}\triangleq g_{Z}(Z_{i},Z_{j})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Z}(Z_{i},Z_{j^{\prime}}).

(460)

For
i,j\in[s]
, we also define

\textstyle(R_{ijji},\,R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))})=\sum_{a\in\mathcal{I}_{i},b\in\mathcal{I}_{j}}(\bar{K}_{ab}\bar{L}_{ba},\,\bar{K}_{ab}\bar{L}_{\texttt{swap}(b)\texttt{swap}(a)})

(461)

\textstyle(R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i},\,R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))})=\sum_{a\in\mathcal{I}_{i},b\in\mathcal{I}_{j}}(\bar{K}_{ab}\bar{L}_{\texttt{swap}(b)a},\,\bar{K}_{ab}\bar{L}_{b\texttt{swap}(a)})

(462)

where the bins
\mathcal{I}_{1},\ldots,\mathcal{I}_{s}
and the mapping
swap
are defined in
Alg.

2
. Note the similarity between
R_{ijkl}
and the quantities
S_{ijkl}
from
Alg.

2
.
Now define the V-statistic

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}\triangleq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\bar{K}_{ij}\bar{L}_{ji}=\frac{1}{s^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{s}R_{ijji}

(463)

and its cheaply permuted version for any wild bootstrap permutation
\pi
on
[s]
,

\textstyle\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}\triangleq\frac{1}{s^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{s}R_{ij\pi_{j}\pi_{i}}.

(464)

Our next result shows the close relationship between
V
and
\tilde{V}
.

Lemma 20

(V-statistic relationship)
.

In the notation of
463
,
464
, and
Def.

3

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}=\frac{1}{4}V_{n}\quad\text{and}\quad\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}=\frac{1}{4}V^{\pi,s}_{n}

(465)

for any wild bootstrap permutation
\pi
on
[s]
.

Proof.

It suffices to show the result for
\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}
.
We expand
V^{\pi,s}_{n}
:

\textstyle V^{\pi,s}_{n}

\textstyle=\frac{1}{n^{4}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4})\in[n]^{4}}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{2}})+g_{Y}(Y_{i_{3}},Y_{i_{4}})-g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{3}})-g_{Y}(Y_{i_{2}},Y_{i_{4}})\big)

(466)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{1}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}})+g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{3}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{4}}})-g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{1}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{3}}})-g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{4}}})\big)

(467)

\textstyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in[n]^{2}}\bigg(g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{2}})g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{1}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}})-g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{2}})\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{3}\in[n]}g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{3}}})\big)

(468)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{4}\in[n]}g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{4}})\big)g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{1}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}})

(469)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{3}\in[n]}g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{3}}})\big)\big(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{4}\in[n]}g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{4}})\big)\bigg)

(470)

\textstyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in[n]^{2}}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{2}})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{4}\in[n]}g_{Y}(Y_{i_{1}},Y_{i_{4}})\big)

(471)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{1}}})-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{3}\in[n]}g_{Z}(Z_{\pi_{i_{2}}},Z_{\pi_{i_{3}}})\big)

(472)

\textstyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}}\sum_{(i_{1},i_{2})\in[n]^{2}}\bar{K}_{i_{1},i_{2}}\bar{L}_{\pi_{i_{2}},\pi_{i_{1}}}=4\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}.

(473)

∎

G.3
Bounding the fluctuations of the threshold

We next identify CQBs
\Psi_{\mathbb{X},s}
and
\Psi_{n,s}
that satisfy the properties

\textstyle\Pr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(V^{\pi,s}_{n}\geq\Psi_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\mid\mathbb{X}}}\right)\leq\alpha,\qquad\forall\alpha\in(0,1),\quad\text{and}\quad

(474)

\textstyle\Pr(\Psi_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\geq\Psi_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta))\leq\beta,\qquad\forall\alpha,\beta\in(0,1)

(475)

for the cheaply permuted V-statistic
V^{\pi,s}_{n}

93
.
Since the alternative V-statistic representation
\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}

464
, satisfies
\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}=\frac{1}{4}V^{\pi,s}_{n}
by
Lem.

20
, we will find
\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s}
and
\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}
satisfying

\textstyle\mathrm{Pr}(\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}\geq\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\mid\mathbb{X})\leq\alpha,\qquad\forall\alpha\in(0,1),\quad\text{and}\quad

(476)

\textstyle\mathrm{Pr}(\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\geq\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta))\leq\beta,\qquad\forall\alpha,\beta\in(0,1),

(477)

and set
\Psi_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)=4\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)
and
\Psi_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta)=4\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta)
.
Our derivation makes use of four lemmas.
The first, proved in
Sec.

G.5
, rewrites
\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}
in terms of independent Rademacher random variables.
Below,

Lemma 21

(Rademacher representation of
\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}
)
.

Instantiate the assumptions of
Thm.

4
, and let

\|{\cdot}\|_{F}
represent the Frobenius norm,
\mathbf{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{s}
be a vector of ones, and

\textstyle\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)\triangleq i+\frac{s}{2}-s\mathbf{1}_{i>\frac{s}{2}}

(478)

for each
i\in[s]
.
If
\pi
is a wild bootstrap permutation on
[s]
drawn independently of
\mathbb{X}
, then the alternative V-statistic representation

464
satisfies

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}=\epsilon^{\top}A\epsilon+b^{\top}\epsilon+c,

(479)

where
\epsilon\sim\textnormal{Unif}(\{\pm 1\}^{s/2})

is independent of
\mathbb{X}
, and
A\in\mathbb{R}^{s/2\times s/2}
,
b\in\mathbb{R}^{s/2}
, and
c\in\mathbb{R}
satisfy

\textstyle\big\|A-\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{s^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\big\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{(\mathcal{A}^{(1)})^{2}(\mathcal{A}^{(2)})^{2}}{4s^{4}}+\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})((\mathcal{A}^{(1)})^{2}+(\mathcal{A}^{(2)})^{2})}{4s^{3}},

(480)

\textstyle\mathrm{Tr}\big(A-\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{s^{2}}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top}\big)\leq\frac{\mathcal{A}^{(1)}\mathcal{A}^{(2)}}{2s^{2}}+\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})(\mathcal{A}^{(1)}+\mathcal{A}^{(2)})}{4s^{3/2}},

(481)

\textstyle\big|c-\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{4}\big|\leq\frac{1}{4}\big(\frac{(\mathcal{A}^{(3)})^{2}}{s}+\frac{2\mathcal{A}^{(3)}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{\sqrt{s}}\big),\text{ and }

(482)

\textstyle\big\|b-\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{s}\mathbf{1}\big\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{3(\mathcal{A}^{(2)})^{2}(\mathcal{A}^{(3)})^{2}+(\mathcal{A}^{(1)})^{2}(\mathcal{A}^{(4)})^{2}}{4s^{3}}

(483)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{3\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})((\mathcal{A}^{(1)})^{2}+(\mathcal{A}^{(2)})^{2}+(\mathcal{A}^{(3)})^{2}+(\mathcal{A}^{(4)})^{2})}{4s^{2}}

(484)

for the random variables

\textstyle\mathcal{A}^{(1)}

\textstyle\triangleq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{s}\|(\mu_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}},\quad\mathcal{A}^{(2)}\triangleq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{s}\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}},

(485)

\textstyle\mathcal{A}^{(3)}

\textstyle\triangleq\sqrt{s}\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}},\quad\text{and}\quad\mathcal{A}^{(4)}\triangleq\sqrt{s}\|(\tilde{\mu}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}

(486)

defined in terms of the random signed measures

\textstyle\mu_{i}\triangleq\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}+\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}},\ \ \tilde{\mu}_{i}\triangleq\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}},

(487)

\textstyle\mu\triangleq{\delta_{\mathbb{X}}}-2{\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}}\times{\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}}+\frac{1}{s}\sum_{j=1}^{s}{\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}}\times{\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}},\ \ \text{and}\ \ \tilde{\mu}\triangleq{\delta_{\mathbb{X}}}-\frac{1}{s}\sum_{j=1}^{s}{\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}}\times{\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}}.

(488)

Our second lemma, proved in
Sec.

G.6
, bounds the expectation of each quantity
\mathcal{A}^{(i)}
introduced in
Lem.

21
.

Lemma 22

(
\mathcal{A}^{(i)}
mean bounds)
.

Under the assumptions and notation of
Lem.

21
with
m\triangleq\frac{n}{s}
,

\textstyle\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{A}^{(1)}\big]

\textstyle\leq D_{1}\triangleq\sqrt{Ks}\big(\frac{5}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\big),\quad\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{A}^{(2)}\big]\leq D_{2}\triangleq\sqrt{Ks}\,\frac{3}{\sqrt{m}},

(489)

\textstyle\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{A}^{(3)}\big]

\textstyle\leq D_{3}\triangleq\sqrt{Ks}\,\frac{5+\sqrt{8}}{\sqrt{n}},\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{A}^{(4)}\big]\leq D_{4}\triangleq\sqrt{Ks}\,\frac{1+\sqrt{8}}{\sqrt{n}}.

(490)

Our third lemma, proved in
Sec.

G.7
, uses
Lem.

22
and the bounded differences inequality (
Lem.

18
) to provide CQBs for each quantity
\mathcal{A}^{(i)}
introduced in
Lem.

21
.

Lemma 23

(
\mathcal{A}^{(i)}
complementary quantile bounds)
.

Instantiate the assumptions and notation of
Lem.

22
, and fix any
i\in[4]
.
With probability at least
1-\delta
,

\textstyle\mathcal{A}^{(i)}

\textstyle\leq D_{i}+C_{i}\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\delta)}{2}}\quad\text{for}\quad(C_{1},C_{2},C_{3},C_{4})=(\frac{10\sqrt{Ks}}{\sqrt{m}}+\frac{2\sqrt{Ks}}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{6\sqrt{Ks}}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{14\sqrt{Ks}}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{6\sqrt{Ks}}{\sqrt{n}}).

(491)

Our fourth lemma, proved in
Sec.

G.8
, uses the estimates of
Lem.

23
to verify the CQB properties
476
and
477
for a particular pairing of
(\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s},\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s})
.

Lemma 24

(Quantile bound for independence permutation threshold)
.

Instantiate the assumptions and notation of
Lem.

23
.
Then, for all
\alpha,\beta\in(0,1)
,

\textstyle\frac{1}{4}\Psi_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta)

\textstyle\triangleq\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta)

(492)

\textstyle\triangleq\frac{K}{n}(196+148\sqrt{\log(4/\beta)}+48\log(4/\beta))\big(\frac{3}{4}+\frac{5\log(3/\alpha)}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha)}\big)

(493)

\textstyle+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{n}}\big(30+22\sqrt{\log(4/\beta)}\big)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1+12\log(3/\alpha)}{6\sqrt{s}}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha)}\big)

(494)

\textstyle+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big(\frac{1}{4}+\frac{\log(6/\alpha)}{s}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(6/\alpha)}{s}}\big)

(495)

satisfies
\Pr(\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\geq\tilde{\Psi}_{n,s}(\alpha,\beta))\leq\beta
for
\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)
defined in equation (
G.8.1
) and satisfying
\Pr(\tilde{V}^{\pi,s}_{n}\geq\tilde{\Psi}_{\mathbb{X},s}(\alpha)\mid\mathbb{X})\leq\alpha
.

G.4
Putting everything together

Fix any
\beta\in(0,1)
, and suppose that

\textstyle\mathcal{T}=4\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\geq\Psi_{n,s}(\alpha^{\star},\beta/3)+\Phi_{n}(\beta/3),

(496)

for
\Psi_{n,s}
and
\Phi_{n}
defined in
Lems.

24
and
19
.
By
Lems.

24
,
20
,
19
, and
2
, a cheap wild bootstrap test based on
V_{n}
has power at least
1-\beta
.
We now show that the assumed condition of
Thm.

4
is sufficient to imply
496
.
Indeed, we see that
496
holds whenever
ax^{2}-bx-c\geq 0
for
x=\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}),

\textstyle a

\textstyle=\frac{3}{4}-\frac{\log(6/\alpha^{\star})}{s}-\sqrt{\frac{\log(6/\alpha^{\star})}{s}},

(497)

\textstyle b

\textstyle=\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{n}}\big(30+22\sqrt{\log(12/\beta)}\big)\big(3+\frac{1+12\log({3}{/\alpha^{\star}})}{6\sqrt{s}}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}\big)

(498)

\textstyle\geq\frac{\sqrt{K}}{\sqrt{n}}\big(30+22\sqrt{\log(12/\beta)}\big)\big(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1+12\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}{6\sqrt{s}}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}\big)+\frac{6\sqrt{K}(1+\sqrt{2\log(3/\beta)})}{\sqrt{n}}

(499)

\textstyle c

\textstyle=\frac{K}{n}(14+7\sqrt{\log(12/\beta)})^{2}\big(\sqrt{\frac{7}{8}}+\sqrt{\frac{5}{3}\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}\big)^{2}

(500)

\textstyle\geq\frac{K}{n}(14+7\sqrt{\log(12/\beta)})^{2}\big(\frac{7}{8}+\frac{5\log({3}{/\alpha^{\star}})}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}\big)

(501)

\textstyle\geq\frac{K}{n}(196+148\sqrt{\log(12/\beta)}+48\log(12/\beta))\big(\frac{3}{4}+\frac{5\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}{3}+\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}\log(3/\alpha^{\star})}\big)

(502)

\textstyle\qquad+\frac{3K}{n}(1+\sqrt{2\log(3/\beta)})^{2}

(503)

Since
a\leq 1
, it suffices to have

x\geq\frac{1}{a}(b+\sqrt{c})\geq\frac{b}{a}+\sqrt{\frac{c}{a}}\geq\frac{b+\sqrt{b^{2}+4ac}}{2a}
.
The advertised result now follows as

3+\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{7}{8}}\leq\frac{7}{2}
and
\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{5}{3}}\leq 2
.

G.5
Proof of

Lem.

21
:
Lemma 21

(Rademacher representation of
\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}
)
.

Recall the definition
461
of
R_{ijkl}
, and, for each
i\in[s]
, define the Rademacher vector
\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}=2\mathbf{1}_{i=\pi_{i}}-1
. Observe that by construction,
\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}=\tilde{\epsilon}_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)}
for all
i\in[s/2]
. Then

\textstyle R_{ij\pi_{j}\pi_{i}}

\textstyle=(\frac{1+\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{2})(\frac{1+\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{2})R_{ijji}+(\frac{1+\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{2})(\frac{1-\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{2})R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}

(504)

\textstyle\quad+(\frac{1-\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{2})(\frac{1+\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{2})R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+(\frac{1-\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{2})(\frac{1-\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{2})R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}

(505)

\textstyle=\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(506)

\textstyle\quad+\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(507)

\textstyle\quad+\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}+R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(508)

\textstyle\quad+\frac{1}{4}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}+R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big).

(509)

Hence,

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}

\textstyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{s}\big(\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(510)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{i}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(511)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tilde{\epsilon}_{j}}{4}\big(R_{ijji}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}+R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)

(512)

\textstyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{4}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}+R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big)\big)

(513)

\textstyle\triangleq\tilde{\epsilon}^{\top}A\tilde{\epsilon}+b^{\top}\tilde{\epsilon}+c,

(514)

where
\tilde{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{s\times s}
,
\tilde{b}\in\mathbb{R}^{s}
, and
\tilde{c}\in\mathbb{R}
have the components

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\tilde{a}_{ij}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\big(R_{ijji}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big),\\
\textstyle\tilde{b}_{i}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}-R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}-R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\quad+R_{jiij}+R_{ji(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))j}-R_{jii(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}-R_{ji(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}\big),\\
\textstyle\tilde{c}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4n^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{s}\big(R_{ijji}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}+R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}+R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}\big).\end{split}

We now rewrite the terms
R_{ijji}
,
R_{ijqi}
,
R_{ijjr}
, and
R_{ijqr}
in terms of kernel expectations:

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}R_{ijji}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{m}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{m(j-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{j^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{m(j-1)+k},Z_{m(i-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Z}(Z_{m(j-1)+k},Z_{i^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=((\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(j)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(j)}})\times(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}))\mathbf{k},\end{split}

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}R_{ijqi}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{m}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{m(j-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{j^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{m(q-1)+k},Z_{m(i-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Z}(Z_{m(q-1)+k},Z_{i^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=((\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(q)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(q)}})\times(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}))\mathbf{k},\end{split}

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}R_{ijjr}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{m}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{m(j-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{j^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{m(j-1)+k},Z_{m(r-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Z}(Z_{m(j-1)+k},Z_{i^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=((\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(j)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(j)}})\times(\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(r)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}))\mathbf{k},\quad\text{and}\quad\end{split}

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\frac{1}{m^{2}}R_{ijqr}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{m^{2}}\sum_{k,l=1}^{m}\big(g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{m(j-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Y}(Y_{m(i-1)+k},Y_{j^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\times\big(g_{Z}(Z_{m(q-1)+k},Z_{m(r-1)+l})-n^{-1}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n}g_{Z}(Z_{m(q-1)+k},Z_{i^{\prime}})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=((\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(q)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(q)}})\times(\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(r)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}))\mathbf{k}.\end{split}

Plugging
q=\texttt{swap}_{s}(j)
and
r=\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)
in

,
Sec.

G.5
,
Sec.

G.5
,
Sec.

G.5
, we obtain

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\tilde{a}_{ij}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big(\frac{R_{ijji}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}\big)=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}(\mu_{j}\times\tilde{\mu}_{i})\mathbf{k}.\end{split}

We bound the following quantity using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality:

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle&\textstyle\big|\tilde{a}_{ij}-\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big|\langle\mu_{j}\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}-\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big|\langle\mu_{j}\mathbf{k}-(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad-\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}-\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big|\langle\mu_{j}\mathbf{k}-(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k}-(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad+\langle\mu_{j}\mathbf{k}-(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad-\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}-\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big(\|(\mu_{j}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big(\|(\mu_{j}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big)\big).\end{split}

Plugging
q=\texttt{swap}_{s}(j)
and
r=\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)
into

G.5
,
Sec.

G.5
,
Sec.

G.5
,
Sec.

G.5
, we find that

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\tilde{b}_{i}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{s}\big(\frac{R_{ijji}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\frac{R_{jiij}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ji(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))j}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{jii(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}{m^{2}}-\frac{R_{ji(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}{m^{2}}\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big(\big\langle\sum_{j=1}^{s}(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(j)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(j)}}+\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}})\mathbf{k},\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}+\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}})\mathbf{k}\big\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\big\langle(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(i)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(i)}}+\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}})\mathbf{k},\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\sum_{j=1}^{s}(\delta_{\mathbb{X}^{(j)}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}-\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}}+\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(j)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}})\mathbf{k}\big\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s}(\langle\mu\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}+\langle\mu_{i}\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}).\end{split}

We also bound the following using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality:

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle&\textstyle\big|\tilde{b}_{i}-\frac{1}{2s}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s}\big|\langle\mu\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}_{i}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}+\langle\mu_{i}\mathbf{k},\tilde{\mu}\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}-2\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq\frac{1}{4s}\big(\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad+\|(\mu_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\|(\tilde{\mu}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big(\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\|(\mu_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big)\big).\end{split}

Plugging

, we find that

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\tilde{c}&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{s}\big(\frac{R_{ijji}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))i}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ijj(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}+\frac{R_{ij(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}{m^{2}}\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4}\big\|\big(\delta_{\mathbb{X}}-2\delta_{\mathbb{Y}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}}+\sum_{i=1}^{s}\delta_{\mathbb{Y}^{(i)}}\times\delta_{\mathbb{Z}^{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))}}\big)\mathbf{k}\big\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}=\frac{1}{4}\|\mu\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}\end{split}

We also bound the scalar
\tilde{c}-\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})
using Cauchy-Schwarz:

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle&\textstyle\big|\tilde{c}-\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big|=\big|\frac{1}{4}\|\mu\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4}\big|\langle(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k},\mu\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}-\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mu)\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4}\big|\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|^{2}_{\mathbf{k}}-2\langle(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}-\mu)\mathbf{k},(\Pi-\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\rangle_{\mathbf{k}}\big|\\
\textstyle&\textstyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\big(\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}+2\|(\mu-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big)\\
\textstyle&\textstyle=\frac{1}{4}\big(\frac{(\mathcal{A}^{(3)})^{2}}{s}+\frac{2\mathcal{A}^{(3)}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{\sqrt{s}}\big).\end{split}

At this point, we further rewrite the expression
\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}=\tilde{\epsilon}^{\top}\tilde{A}\tilde{\epsilon}+\tilde{b}^{\top}\tilde{\epsilon}+\tilde{c}
in terms of
\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}
. Since
\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}=\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}
, we have that

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}

\textstyle=\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}^{\top}\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,1:s/2}\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}+\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}^{\top}\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,1:s/2}\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}

(515)

\textstyle\quad+\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}^{\top}\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,s/2:s}\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}+\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}^{\top}\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,s/2:s}\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}

(516)

\textstyle\quad+\tilde{b}_{1:s/2}^{\top}\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}+\tilde{b}_{s/2:s}^{\top}\tilde{\epsilon}_{s/2:s}+\tilde{c}

(517)

\textstyle=\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}^{\top}\big(\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,1:s/2}+\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,1:s/2}+\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,s/2:s}+\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,s/2:s}\big)\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}

(518)

\textstyle\quad+\big(\tilde{b}_{1:s/2}+\tilde{b}_{s/2:s}\big)^{\top}\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}+\tilde{c}.

(519)

Thus, if we define

\textstyle\epsilon

\textstyle=\tilde{\epsilon}_{1:s/2}\sim\textnormal{Unif}(\{\pm 1\}^{s/2}),

(520)

\textstyle A

\textstyle=\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,1:s/2}+\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,1:s/2}+\tilde{A}_{1:s/2,s/2:s}+\tilde{A}_{s/2:s,s/2:s}\in\mathbb{R}^{s/2\times s/2},

(521)

\textstyle b

\textstyle=\tilde{b}_{1:s/2}+\tilde{b}_{s/2:s}\in\mathbb{R}^{s/2},\quad\text{and}\quad c=\tilde{c}\in\mathbb{R},

(522)

we have that

\textstyle\tilde{V}_{n}^{\pi,s}=\epsilon^{\top}A\epsilon+b^{\top}\epsilon+c.

(523)

If we let
a_{ij}
and

be the components of

and

, we have that

\textstyle a_{ij}

\textstyle=\tilde{a}_{ij}+\tilde{a}_{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))j}+\tilde{a}_{i(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}+\tilde{a}_{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}\quad\text{and}\quad b_{i}=\tilde{b}_{i}+\tilde{b}_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)}.

(524)

Next, define

\textstyle\begin{split}\textstyle\bar{A}&\textstyle=A-\frac{1}{s^{2}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top},\\
\textstyle\bar{b}&\textstyle=b-\frac{\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})}{s}\mathbf{1}\mathbf{1}^{\top},\quad\text{and}\quad\\
\textstyle\bar{c}&\textstyle=c-\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q}).\end{split}

If we let

and

be the components of

and
\bar{b}
, by equations
Sec.

G.5
and
Sec.

G.5
, we have that

\textstyle\big|\bar{a}_{ij}\big|

(525)

\textstyle+\big|\tilde{a}_{i(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}-\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big|+\big|\tilde{a}_{(\texttt{swap}_{s}(i))(\texttt{swap}_{s}(j))}-\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}^{2}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big|

(526)

\textstyle\leq\frac{1}{4s^{2}}\big(\big(\|(\mu_{j}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\mu_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(j)}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big)

(527)

\textstyle\qquad\quad\times\big(\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big)

(528)

\textstyle\quad+\mathrm{MMD}_{\mathbf{k}}(\Pi,\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\big(\|(\mu_{j}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{i}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}

(529)

\textstyle\qquad\quad+\|(\mu_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(j)}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}+\|(\tilde{\mu}_{\texttt{swap}_{s}(i)}-\Pi+\mathbb{P}\times\mathbb{Q})\mathbf{k}\|_{\mathbf{k}}\big)\big)

(530)

and

(531)

(533)
